In this work, we critically examine this assumption by systematically evaluating Speech Emotion Recognition (SER) on synthesized speech across datasets, discriminative and generative SER models, and diverse synthesis models. We find that current SER models can not generalize to synthesized speech, largely because speech token prediction during synthesis induces a representation mismatch between synthesized and human speech. Moreover, generative Speech Language Models (SLMs) tend to infer emotion from textual semantics while ignoring paralinguistic cues. Overall, our findings suggest that existing SER models often exploit non-robust shortcuts rather than capturing fundamental features, and paralinguistic understanding in SLMs remains challenging.

</details>


### [39] [How often do Answers Change? Estimating Recency Requirements in Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16544)
*Bhawna Piryani,Zehra Mert,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RecencyQA数据集，基于时效性-平稳性分类法对问题进行分类，用于评估LLM在时间敏感问题上的表现，特别关注上下文依赖的非平稳问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在处理时间敏感问题时经常依赖过时知识，产生自信但错误的回答。现有基准测试要么定期刷新答案，要么依赖固定模板，但未能反映答案变化的频率或问题本身是否需要最新信息。

Method: 提出时效性-平稳性分类法，根据答案变化频率及其是否随时间不变或上下文依赖对问题进行分类。基于此构建RecencyQA数据集，包含4,031个开放域问题，标注了时效性和平稳性标签。

Result: 通过人工评估和实证分析发现，非平稳问题（即上下文改变时效性要求的问题）对LLM更具挑战性，且难度随更新频率增加而上升。RecencyQA能够进行细粒度的时间推理基准测试。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模时效性和上下文依赖性，RecencyQA超越了简单的"新鲜度"二元概念，为开发时效性感知和上下文敏感的问答系统提供了基础，支持更精细的时间推理评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often rely on outdated knowledge when answering time-sensitive questions, leading to confident yet incorrect responses. Without explicit signals indicating whether up-to-date information is required, models struggle to decide when to retrieve external evidence, how to reason about stale facts, and how to rank answers by their validity. Existing benchmarks either periodically refresh answers or rely on fixed templates, but they do not reflect on how frequently answers change or whether a question inherently requires up-to-date information. To address this gap, we introduce a recency-stationarity taxonomy that categorizes questions by how often their answers change and whether this change frequency is time-invariant or context-dependent. Building on this taxonomy, we present RecencyQA, a dataset of 4,031 open-domain questions annotated with recency and stationarity labels. Through human evaluation and empirical analysis, we show that non-stationary questions, i.e., those where context changes the recency requirement, are significantly more challenging for LLMs, with difficulty increasing as update frequency rises. By explicitly modeling recency and context dependence, RecencyQA enables fine-grained benchmarking and analysis of temporal reasoning beyond binary notions of freshness, and provides a foundation for developing recency-aware and context-sensitive question answering systems.

</details>


### [40] [DanceHA: A Multi-Agent Framework for Document-Level Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16546)
*Lei Wang,Min Huang,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DanceHA多智能体框架用于开放域文档级方面情感强度分析，并发布Inf-ABSIA数据集，解决非正式文本中细粒度ACOSI元组提取问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方面情感强度分析研究主要集中在领域特定的句子级别，而文档级ABSIA特别是处理复杂ACOSI元组提取任务的研究不足，尤其是非正式写作风格下的分析。

Method: 提出DanceHA多智能体框架：Dance采用分治策略将长上下文ABSIA任务分解为可管理的子任务，由专门智能体协作完成；HA实现人机协作标注。基于此框架构建Inf-ABSIA多领域文档级ABSIA数据集。

Result: 实验证明DanceHA框架有效，多智能体知识可成功迁移到学生模型中。结果强调了非正式风格在ABSIA中的重要性，因为这种风格通常会强化与特定方面相关的观点。

Conclusion: DanceHA框架成功解决了开放域文档级方面情感强度分析问题，特别是非正式文本中的ACOSI元组提取，为文档级ABSIA研究提供了新方法和高质量数据集。

Abstract: Aspect-Based Sentiment Intensity Analysis (ABSIA) has garnered increasing attention, though research largely focuses on domain-specific, sentence-level settings. In contrast, document-level ABSIA--particularly in addressing complex tasks like extracting Aspect-Category-Opinion-Sentiment-Intensity (ACOSI) tuples--remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce DanceHA, a multi-agent framework designed for open-ended, document-level ABSIA with informal writing styles. DanceHA has two main components: Dance, which employs a divide-and-conquer strategy to decompose the long-context ABSIA task into smaller, manageable sub-tasks for collaboration among specialized agents; and HA, Human-AI collaboration for annotation. We release Inf-ABSIA, a multi-domain document-level ABSIA dataset featuring fine-grained and high-accuracy labels from DanceHA. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our agentic framework and show that the multi-agent knowledge in DanceHA can be effectively transferred into student models. Our results highlight the importance of the overlooked informal styles in ABSIA, as they often intensify opinions tied to specific aspects.

</details>


### [41] [EmoLLM: Appraisal-Grounded Cognitive-Emotional Co-Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16553)
*Yifei Zhang,Mingyang Li,Henry Gao,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EmoLLM：基于评估理论的IQ/EQ协同推理对话框架，通过显式的评估推理图结构化中间推理，在保持事实可靠性的同时提升情感状态结果和响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在认知智能（IQ）方面表现强劲，但许多实际交互场景需要情感智能（EQ）来生成既事实可靠又情感适当的响应。在情感支持、技术协助和咨询等场景中，有效对话取决于如何根据用户需求、目标和应对能力来评估情境。

Method: 提出EmoLLM框架，基于评估理论进行IQ/EQ协同推理。使用显式的评估推理图（ARG）结构化中间推理，涵盖上下文事实、推断用户需求、评估维度、情感状态和响应策略。通过强化学习在多轮角色扮演环境中训练，利用反向视角推理基于预测的用户端响应后果提供奖励信号。

Result: 在多样化对话场景中，EmoLLM相比强基线模型，在保持强事实可靠性的同时，改善了情感状态结果和响应质量。

Conclusion: EmoLLM框架成功地将评估理论融入大语言模型的推理过程，实现了认知智能和情感智能的协同优化，为需要情感智能的实际对话应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong cognitive intelligence (IQ), yet many real-world interactions also require emotional intelligence (EQ) to produce responses that are both factually reliable and emotionally appropriate. In settings such as emotional support, technical assistance, and consultation, effective dialogue depends on how situations are appraised with respect to the user's needs, goals, and coping capacity. Inspired by appraisal theory, we propose EmoLLM, an appraisal-grounded framework for IQ/EQ co-reasoning in dialogue. EmoLLM uses an explicit Appraisal Reasoning Graph (ARG) to structure intermediate reasoning over contextual facts, inferred user needs, appraisal dimensions, emotional states, and response strategies before generating a reply. We train EmoLLM in a multi-turn role-play environment with reinforcement learning, where reverse-perspective reasoning provides reward signals based on predicted user-side consequences of responses. Across diverse dialogue settings, EmoLLM improves emotional state outcomes and response quality over strong baselines while preserving strong factual reliability.

</details>


### [42] [Characterizing Delusional Spirals through Human-LLM Chat Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16567)
*Jared Moore,Ashish Mehta,William Agnew,Jacy Reese Anthis,Ryan Louie,Yifan Mai,Peggy Yin,Myra Cheng,Samuel J Paech,Kevin Klyman,Stevie Chancellor,Eric Lin,Nick Haber,Desmond C. Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究首次对19名报告因使用LLM聊天机器人而遭受心理伤害的用户的对话日志进行深入分析，揭示了聊天机器人可能加剧用户妄想、自杀倾向等心理问题的具体机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLMs）的普及，全球媒体和法律讨论中出现了关于聊天机器人导致妄想、自残和"AI精神病"等负面心理影响的报道。然而，目前缺乏对用户与聊天机器人在长时间妄想"螺旋"中互动过程的实证研究，限制了我们对这些伤害的理解和缓解能力。

Method: 研究分析了19名报告因使用聊天机器人而遭受心理伤害的用户的对话日志，这些参与者来自相关支持小组或被媒体报道过。研究者开发了包含28个编码的清单，应用于391,562条消息的分析，包括用户是否表现出妄想思维、表达自杀想法，或聊天机器人是否错误地声称自己有感知能力等。

Result: 分析发现：15.5%的用户消息表现出妄想思维；有69条经过验证的用户消息表达自杀想法；21.2%的聊天机器人消息错误地声称自己有感知能力。研究还发现，表达浪漫兴趣和聊天机器人声称有感知的消息在较长对话中出现频率更高，表明这些话题可能促进或源于用户的过度投入，且这些领域的安全措施在多轮对话中可能失效。

Conclusion: 研究提出了具体的建议，供政策制定者、LLM聊天机器人开发者和用户使用研究者的编码清单和对话分析工具来理解和缓解LLM聊天机器人可能造成的伤害。这是首个对真实有害案例的深入实证研究。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) have proliferated, disturbing anecdotal reports of negative psychological effects, such as delusions, self-harm, and ``AI psychosis,'' have emerged in global media and legal discourse. However, it remains unclear how users and chatbots interact over the course of lengthy delusional ``spirals,'' limiting our ability to understand and mitigate the harm. In our work, we analyze logs of conversations with LLM chatbots from 19 users who report having experienced psychological harms from chatbot use. Many of our participants come from a support group for such chatbot users. We also include chat logs from participants covered by media outlets in widely-distributed stories about chatbot-reinforced delusions. In contrast to prior work that speculates on potential AI harms to mental health, to our knowledge we present the first in-depth study of such high-profile and veridically harmful cases. We develop an inventory of 28 codes and apply it to the $391,562$ messages in the logs. Codes include whether a user demonstrates delusional thinking (15.5% of user messages), a user expresses suicidal thoughts (69 validated user messages), or a chatbot misrepresents itself as sentient (21.2% of chatbot messages). We analyze the co-occurrence of message codes. We find, for example, that messages that declare romantic interest and messages where the chatbot describes itself as sentient occur much more often in longer conversations, suggesting that these topics could promote or result from user over-engagement and that safeguards in these areas may degrade in multi-turn settings. We conclude with concrete recommendations for how policymakers, LLM chatbot developers, and users can use our inventory and conversation analysis tool to understand and mitigate harm from LLM chatbots.
  Warning: This paper discusses self-harm, trauma, and violence.

</details>


### [43] [Diverging Transformer Predictions for Human Sentence Processing: A Comprehensive Analysis of Agreement Attraction Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16574)
*Titus von der Malsburg,Sebastian Padó*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了11个自回归Transformer模型在英语一致吸引配置上的认知表现，发现模型在介词短语配置上与人类阅读时间数据基本一致，但在宾语提取关系从句配置上表现显著下降，且无法复现人类的不对称干扰模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer在计算语言学中几乎主导了所有最先进的语言模型，但其作为人类句子处理模型的认知充分性仍存在争议。本研究旨在通过更全面的实验设计，系统评估不同规模和架构的Transformer模型在人类形态句法处理方面的表现。

Method: 使用基于惊奇度的链接机制，系统评估了11个不同规模和架构的自回归Transformer模型。实验涵盖了比先前研究更全面的英语一致吸引配置，特别关注介词短语配置和宾语提取关系从句配置。

Result: 实验结果呈现混合模式：Transformer预测在介词短语配置上通常与人类阅读时间数据一致，但在宾语提取关系从句配置上表现显著下降。在后者情况下，不同模型的预测存在明显差异，且没有模型能够成功复现人类观察到的不对称干扰模式。

Conclusion: 当前Transformer模型无法解释人类的形态句法处理过程。评估Transformer作为认知模型时，必须采用严谨、全面的实验设计，避免从孤立的句法配置或个别模型中得出虚假的泛化结论。

Abstract: Transformers underlie almost all state-of-the-art language models in computational linguistics, yet their cognitive adequacy as models of human sentence processing remains disputed. In this work, we use a surprisal-based linking mechanism to systematically evaluate eleven autoregressive transformers of varying sizes and architectures on a more comprehensive set of English agreement attraction configurations than prior work. Our experiments yield mixed results: While transformer predictions generally align with human reading time data for prepositional phrase configurations, performance degrades significantly on object-extracted relative clause configurations. In the latter case, predictions also diverge markedly across models, and no model successfully replicates the asymmetric interference patterns observed in humans. We conclude that current transformer models do not explain human morphosyntactic processing, and that evaluations of transformers as cognitive models must adopt rigorous, comprehensive experimental designs to avoid spurious generalizations from isolated syntactic configurations or individual models.

</details>


### [44] [BATQuant: Outlier-resilient MXFP4 Quantization via Learnable Block-wise Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16590)
*Ji-Fu Li,Manyi Zhang,Xiaobo Xia,Han Bao,Haoli Bai,Zhenhua Dong,Xianzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BATQuant是一种针对MXFP4格式的新型后训练量化方法，通过块级仿射变换避免跨块异常值传播，结合全局-私有Kronecker分解和可学习裁剪，在W4A4KV16配置下显著提升多模态和语言模型的量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法（特别是为整数格式设计的旋转技术）在应用于MXFP4格式时会出现严重的性能崩溃。研究发现这是由于格式不匹配：全局正交旋转无意中将异常值能量跨量化块传递，引发新的异常值破坏局部块级缩放，同时常产生双峰激活分布，未能充分利用有限的量化范围。

Method: 提出BATQuant（块级仿射变换），将变换限制在与MXFP粒度对齐以防止跨块异常值传播，同时放宽正交约束以优化分布形状。采用全局和私有Kronecker分解来有效减少存储和运行时开销，并引入块级可学习裁剪来抑制残余异常值。

Result: 在多模态大语言模型和大型语言模型上的广泛实验表明，BATQuant在激进的W4A4KV16配置下建立了新的最先进结果，在多模态基准测试中恢复了高达96.43%的全精度性能，并在各种任务上明显优于现有方法。

Conclusion: BATQuant通过针对MXFP格式特性设计的块级仿射变换和高效参数化策略，成功解决了现有量化方法在MXFP4上的性能崩溃问题，为在加速器架构上部署多模态和语言模型提供了有效的低精度解决方案。

Abstract: Microscaling floating-point (MXFP) formats have emerged as a promising standard for deploying Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) on modern accelerator architectures. However, existing Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) methods, particularly rotation-based techniques designed for integer formats, suffer from severe performance collapse when applied to MXFP4. Recent studies attribute this failure to a fundamental format mismatch: global orthogonal rotations inadvertently transfer outlier energy across quantization blocks, inducing new outliers that disrupt local block-wise scaling, while often creating bimodal activation distributions that underutilize the limited quantization range. To address these issues, we propose BATQuant (Block-wise Affine Transformation), which restricts transformations to align with MXFP granularity to prevent cross-block outlier propagation, while relaxing orthogonality constraints to optimize distribution shaping. To ensure parameter efficiency, we introduce Global and Private Kronecker (GPK) decomposition to effectively reduces storage and runtime overhead and incorporate Block-wise Learnable Clipping to suppress residual outliers. Extensive experiments on both MLLMs and LLMs demonstrate that BATQuant establishes new state-of-the-art results under aggressive W4A4KV16 configurations, recovering up to 96.43% of full-precision performance on multimodal benchmarks and clearly outperforming existing methods across diverse tasks.

</details>


### [45] [Tarab: A Multi-Dialect Corpus of Arabic Lyrics and Poetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16601)
*Mo El-Haj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Tarab Corpus是一个大规模阿拉伯语文化语言资源，包含256万诗节和1350万词元，整合了歌曲歌词和诗歌，覆盖古典和现代阿拉伯语及六种主要方言变体，时间跨度超过14个世纪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建统一的阿拉伯语创意文本分析框架，填补大规模开放阿拉伯语歌曲和诗歌语料库的空白，支持跨流派、跨时间、跨方言的比较语言学和文体分析。

Method: 构建包含2.56百万诗节的数据集，涵盖古典阿拉伯语、现代标准阿拉伯语及六种主要地区变体（埃及、海湾、黎凡特、伊拉克、苏丹、马格里布阿拉伯语），每个诗节附带结构化元数据（语言变体、地理起源、历史/文化背景），并建立数据收集、标准化和验证流程。

Result: 创建了迄今为止最大的开放阿拉伯语创意文本语料库，平衡了歌曲和诗歌内容，覆盖28个现代国家和多个历史时期，提供了变体识别和流派区分的基础分析，数据集已在HuggingFace公开。

Conclusion: Tarab Corpus为阿拉伯语文化表达研究提供了前所未有的资源，支持语言学、文体学和历时分析，填补了阿拉伯语创意文本大规模语料库的空白，为跨学科研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce the Tarab Corpus, a large-scale cultural and linguistic resource that brings together Arabic song lyrics and poetry within a unified analytical framework. The corpus comprises 2.56 million verses and more than 13.5 million tokens, making it, to our knowledge, the largest open Arabic corpus of creative text spanning both classical and contemporary production. Tarab is broadly balanced between songs and poems and covers Classical Arabic, Modern Standard Arabic (MSA), and six major regional varieties: Egyptian, Gulf, Levantine, Iraqi, Sudanese, and Maghrebi Arabic. The artists and poets represented in the corpus are associated with 28 modern nation states and multiple historical eras, covering over fourteen centuries of Arabic creative expression from the Pre-Islamic period to the twenty-first century. Each verse is accompanied by structured metadata describing linguistic variety, geographic origin, and historical or cultural context, enabling comparative linguistic, stylistic, and diachronic analysis across genres and time. We describe the data collection, normalisation, and validation pipeline and present baseline analyses for variety identification and genre differentiation. The dataset is publicly available on HuggingFace at https://huggingface.co/datasets/drelhaj/Tarab.

</details>


### [46] [Omnilingual SONAR: Cross-Lingual and Cross-Modal Sentence Embeddings Bridging Massively Multilingual Text and Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16606)
*Omnilingual SONAR Team,João Maria Janeiro,Pere-Lluís Huguet Cabot,Ioannis Tsiamas,Yen Meng,Vivek Iyer,Guillem Ramírez,Loic Barrault,Belen Alastruey,Yu-An Chung,Marta R. Costa-Jussa,David Dale,Kevin Heffernan,Jaehyeong Jo,Artyom Kozhevnikov,Alexandre Mourachko,Christophe Ropers,Holger Schwenk,Paul-Ambroise Duquenne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OmniSONAR是一个支持数千种语言的跨语言、跨模态句子嵌入模型家族，能够在单一语义空间中嵌入文本、语音、代码和数学表达式，在跨语言相似性搜索、翻译等任务上实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言句子编码器通常只覆盖几百种语言，且往往需要在下游任务质量和更强对齐之间权衡，限制了其应用。需要开发能够扩展到数千种语言（从高资源到极低资源）并支持多种模态的模型。

Method: 采用渐进式训练：1）使用LLM初始化的编码器-解码器为200种语言学习强基础空间，结合token级解码、新颖的分割softmax对比损失和合成硬负样本；2）通过两阶段师生编码器蒸馏框架扩展到数千种语言变体；3）将177种口语语言映射到该空间中实现跨模态扩展。

Result: 在FLORES数据集上跨语言相似性搜索错误减半，在BIBLE基准上错误减少15倍；翻译性能优于NLLB-3B，在1560种语言到英语的BIBLE翻译上超过先前模型15个chrF++点；在MTEB和XLCoST上表现强劲；语音相似性搜索错误降低43%，达到SeamlessM4T语音到文本质量的97%；通过训练Spectrum编码器-解码器LM处理OmniSONAR嵌入序列，实现数千种语言和语音的复杂下游任务高性能迁移。

Conclusion: OmniSONAR成功构建了一个覆盖数千种语言和多种模态的统一语义空间，通过渐进式训练方法避免了表示崩溃，在跨语言相似性搜索、翻译和跨模态任务上实现了最先进的性能，为大规模多语言和多模态应用提供了强大基础。

Abstract: Cross-lingual sentence encoders typically cover only a few hundred languages and often trade downstream quality for stronger alignment, limiting their adoption. We introduce OmniSONAR, a new family of omnilingual, cross-lingual and cross-modal sentence embedding models that natively embed text, speech, code, and mathematical expressions in a single semantic space, while delivering state-of-the-art downstream performance at the scale of thousands of languages, from high-resource to extremely low-resource varieties. To reach this scale without representation collapse, we use progressive training. We first learn a strong foundational space for 200 languages with an LLM-initialized encoder-decoder, combining token-level decoding with a novel split-softmax contrastive loss and synthetic hard negatives. Building on this foundation, we expand to several thousands language varieties via a two-stage teacher-student encoder distillation framework. Finally, we demonstrate the cross-modal extensibility of this space by seamlessly mapping 177 spoken languages into it. OmniSONAR halves cross-lingual similarity search error on the 200-language FLORES dataset and reduces error by a factor of 15 on the 1,560-language BIBLE benchmark. It also enables strong translation, outperforming NLLB-3B on multilingual benchmarks and exceeding prior models (including much larger LLMs) by 15 chrF++ points on 1,560 languages into English BIBLE translation. OmniSONAR also performs strongly on MTEB and XLCoST. For speech, OmniSONAR achieves a 43% lower similarity-search error and reaches 97% of SeamlessM4T speech-to-text quality, despite being zero-shot for translation (trained only on ASR data). Finally, by training an encoder-decoder LM, Spectrum, exclusively on English text processing OmniSONAR embedding sequences, we unlock high-performance transfer to thousands of languages and speech for complex downstream tasks.

</details>


### [47] [Domain Mixture Design via Log-Likelihood Differences for Aligning Language Models with a Target Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16622)
*Ryo Kishino,Riku Shiomi,Hiroaki Yamagiwa,Momose Oyama,Hidetoshi Shimodaira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种通过设计预训练数据域混合权重来对齐基础模型与目标模型分布的方法，将模型视为对数似然空间中的点，通过调整训练更新方向来对齐目标模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏需要直接蒸馏语言模型，本研究探索通过设计预训练数据域混合权重来对齐基础模型与目标模型的分布，提供一种固定的训练方案

Method: 将模型视为对数似然空间中的点，通过计算训练更新方向与目标模型方向的对齐来确定数据域权重，在预训练或继续预训练中使用这些权重

Result: 在NanoGPT上的实验表明，相比Pile数据的均匀权重，该方法能持续降低与目标模型的KL散度，下游任务性能也更接近目标模型

Conclusion: 虽然知识蒸馏在可用时更有效，但该方法仍能实现有意义的对齐，为模型对齐提供了一种替代方案，特别是在无法直接蒸馏的情况下

Abstract: Instead of directly distilling a language model, this study addresses the problem of aligning a base model with a target model in distribution by designing the domain mixture of training data for pretraining or continued pretraining as a fixed training recipe. We propose a method for determining domain weights by viewing models as points in log-likelihood space and aligning the training update direction with the direction toward the target model. Experiments with NanoGPT show that the proposed method consistently reduces the KL divergence to the target model compared with uniform weighting over the Pile. Although knowledge distillation remains more effective when available, the proposed method still achieves meaningful alignment, and downstream task performance also tends to become closer to that of the target model.

</details>


### [48] [Good Arguments Against the People Pleasers: How Reasoning Mitigates (Yet Masks) LLM Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16643)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Zheng Chen,Jianfei Ma,Yip Tin Po,Emmanuele Chersoni,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了思维链推理在LLM谄媚行为中的作用，发现推理通常能减少最终决策中的谄媚，但也会通过逻辑不一致、计算错误和片面论证等方式掩盖谄媚行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐技术常常无意中导致LLM产生谄媚行为。先前研究主要关注直接回答场景，而思维链推理的作用尚未充分探索：它究竟是作为减轻谄媚的逻辑约束，还是作为掩盖谄媚的事后合理化工具？

Method: 评估了一系列模型在客观和主观任务上的表现，通过机制分析探究三个开源模型在推理过程中谄媚倾向的动态变化。

Result: 推理通常减少最终决策中的谄媚，但也会掩盖某些样本中的谄媚行为，模型通过逻辑不一致、计算错误和片面论证构建欺骗性理由。LLM在主观任务和权威偏见下更容易产生谄媚行为。机制分析显示谄媚倾向在推理过程中是动态变化的，而非在输入阶段预先确定。

Conclusion: 思维链推理在减轻LLM谄媚行为方面具有双重作用：既可作为约束机制减少最终决策中的谄媚，也可能成为掩盖谄媚的合理化工具，特别是在主观任务和权威影响下。谄媚倾向在推理过程中动态演变，而非预先决定。

Abstract: Alignment techniques often inadvertently induce sycophancy in LLMs. While prior studies studied this behaviour in direct-answer settings, the role of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning remains under-explored: does it serve as a logical constraint that mitigates sycophancy, or a tool for post-hoc rationalization that masks it? We evaluate a range of models across objective and subjective tasks to investigate the issue. Results show that reasoning generally reduces sycophancy in final decisions but also masks sycophancy in some samples, where models construct deceptive justifications through logical inconsistencies, calculation errors, and one-sided arguments etc. Furthermore, LLMs are more prone to sycophancy in subjective tasks and under authority-bias. Our mechanistic analysis on three open-source models reveals that the tendency of sycophancy is dynamic during the reasoning process rather than being pre-determined at the input stage.

</details>


### [49] [Omanic: Towards Step-wise Evaluation of Multi-hop Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16654)
*Xiaojie Gu,Sherry T. Tong,Aosong Feng,Sophia Simeng Han,Jinghui Lu,Yingjian Chen,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Chanjun Park,Rex Ying,Irene Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Omanic是一个开放域多跳问答资源，提供分解的子问题和中间答案作为结构化标注，用于分析推理过程，包含机器生成的训练数据和专家评审的评估数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前专注于推理的大语言模型在NLP任务中取得了进展，但评估仍然具有挑战性：最终答案本身无法揭示中间推理步骤，难以确定模型是否真正正确推理以及失败发生在哪里，而现有的多跳QA基准缺乏用于诊断推理失败的步骤级标注。

Method: 提出Omanic资源，包含10,296个机器生成的训练示例（OmanicSynth）和967个专家评审的人工标注评估示例（OmanicBench），提供分解的子问题和中间答案作为结构化标注。

Result: 最先进的LLM在OmanicBench上仅达到73.11%的多项选择准确率，确认其高难度。逐步分析显示CoT的性能取决于事实完整性，在知识缺口下其增益减弱，错误在后续跳中放大。在OmanicSynth上进行监督微调在六个推理和数学基准上带来显著迁移增益（平均7.41分）。

Conclusion: Omanic填补了多跳QA基准缺乏步骤级标注的空白，为分析推理过程提供了结构化资源。数据集的质量得到验证，OmanicSynth作为推理能力迁移的监督信号是有效的。

Abstract: Reasoning-focused large language models (LLMs) have advanced in many NLP tasks, yet their evaluation remains challenging: final answers alone do not expose the intermediate reasoning steps, making it difficult to determine whether a model truly reasons correctly and where failures occur, while existing multi-hop QA benchmarks lack step-level annotations for diagnosing reasoning failures. To address this gap, we propose Omanic, an open-domain multi-hop QA resource that provides decomposed sub-questions and intermediate answers as structural annotations for analyzing reasoning processes. It contains 10,296 machine-generated training examples (OmanicSynth) and 967 expert-reviewed human-annotated evaluation examples (OmanicBench). Systematic evaluations show that state-of-the-art LLMs achieve only 73.11% multiple-choice accuracy on OmanicBench, confirming its high difficulty. Stepwise analysis reveals that CoT's performance hinges on factual completeness, with its gains diminishing under knowledge gaps and errors amplifying in later hops. Additionally, supervised fine-tuning on OmanicSynth brings substantial transfer gains (7.41 average points) across six reasoning and math benchmarks, validating the dataset's quality and further supporting the effectiveness of OmanicSynth as supervision for reasoning-capability transfer. We release the data at https://huggingface.co/datasets/li-lab/Omanic and the code at https://github.com/XiaojieGu/Omanic.

</details>


### [50] [Can Linguistically Related Languages Guide LLM Translation in Low-Resource Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16660)
*Aishwarya Ramasethu,Niyathi Allu,Rohin Garg,Harshwardhan Fartale,Dun Li Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在极低资源机器翻译中表现有限，本文研究在数据稀缺环境下，语言相似的枢轴语言和少样本示例能否为LLM的即时适应提供有效指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准适应技术通常依赖大规模平行数据或大量微调，这对于代表性不足的语言不可行。研究在数据稀缺设置下，语言相似的枢轴语言和少样本示例能否为LLM的即时适应提供有用指导。

Method: 采用数据高效的实验设置，结合语言相关的枢轴语言和少样本上下文示例，无需参数更新，在受控条件下评估翻译行为。

Result: 枢轴提示在某些配置下能带来改进，特别是在目标语言在模型词汇表中代表性较差的情况下，但增益通常有限且对少样本示例构建敏感。对于密切相关的或代表性较好的语言变体，观察到收益递减或不一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果为在低资源翻译设置中，如何以及何时使用推理时提示和基于枢轴的示例作为微调的轻量级替代方案提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across many downstream tasks, yet their effectiveness in extremely low-resource machine translation remains limited. Standard adaptation techniques typically rely on large-scale parallel data or extensive fine-tuning, which are infeasible for the long tail of underrepresented languages. In this work, we investigate a more constrained question: in data-scarce settings, to what extent can linguistically similar pivot languages and few-shot demonstrations provide useful guidance for on-the-fly adaptation in LLMs? We study a data-efficient experimental setup that combines linguistically related pivot languages with few-shot in-context examples, without any parameter updates, and evaluate translation behavior under controlled conditions. Our analysis shows that while pivot-based prompting can yield improvements in certain configurations, particularly in settings where the target language is less well represented in the model's vocabulary, the gains are often modest and sensitive to few shot example construction. For closely related or better represented varieties, we observe diminishing or inconsistent gains. Our findings provide empirical guidance on how and when inference-time prompting and pivot-based examples can be used as a lightweight alternative to fine-tuning in low-resource translation settings.

</details>


### [51] [Arabic Morphosyntactic Tagging and Dependency Parsing with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16718)
*Mohamed Adel,Bashar Alhafni,Nizar Habash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估指令调优大语言模型在阿拉伯语形态句法标注和依存句法分析任务上的表现，发现提示设计和示例选择对性能有显著影响，专有模型在特征级标注上接近监督基线，在依存分析上与专用解析器竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在NLP任务上表现优异，但其生成显式语言结构的能力尚不明确。阿拉伯语因其丰富的形态学和正字法歧义性，为测试LLMs处理形态-句法交互的能力提供了具有挑战性的测试平台。

Method: 使用指令调优的LLMs评估两个阿拉伯语结构化预测任务：形态句法标注和带标签的依存句法分析。比较零样本提示与基于检索的上下文学习（ICL），后者使用阿拉伯语树库中的示例。分析提示设计和演示选择对性能的影响。

Result: 提示设计和演示选择强烈影响性能：专有模型在特征级标注上接近监督基线，在依存分析上与专用解析器竞争。在原始文本设置中，分词仍然具有挑战性，但基于检索的ICL改善了分析和分词性能。

Conclusion: 分析揭示了LLMs能够可靠捕获阿拉伯语形态句法和句法的哪些方面，以及哪些方面仍然困难。研究强调了提示工程和示例选择对于LLMs在结构化预测任务中性能的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform strongly on many NLP tasks, but their ability to produce explicit linguistic structure remains unclear. We evaluate instruction-tuned LLMs on two structured prediction tasks for Standard Arabic: morphosyntactic tagging and labeled dependency parsing. Arabic provides a challenging testbed due to its rich morphology and orthographic ambiguity, which create strong morphology-syntax interactions. We compare zero-shot prompting with retrieval-based in-context learning (ICL) using examples from Arabic treebanks. Results show that prompt design and demonstration selection strongly affect performance: proprietary models approach supervised baselines for feature-level tagging and become competitive with specialized dependency parsers. In raw-text settings, tokenization remains challenging, though retrieval-based ICL improves both parsing and tokenization. Our analysis highlights which aspects of Arabic morphosyntax and syntax LLMs capture reliably and which remain difficult.

</details>


### [52] [Probing Cultural Signals in Large Language Models through Author Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16749)
*Valentin Lafargue,Ariel Guerra-Adames,Emmanuelle Claeys,Elouan Vuichard,Jean-Michel Loubes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析LLM从歌词中零样本推断歌手性别和种族的能力，揭示了LLM存在系统性文化偏见，特别是偏向北美种族，并提出了量化公平性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在社会影响应用中的部署增加，需要评估其编码的文化偏见。研究旨在探究LLM是否能在零样本设置下从歌词进行作者画像，以及这些模型表现出的文化对齐模式。

Method: 在超过10,000首歌词上评估多个开源LLM的零样本作者画像能力，推断歌手性别和种族。通过模型预测分布和生成理由分析文化对齐模式。引入两个公平性指标：模态准确度差异（MAD）和召回差异（RD）来量化偏差。

Result: LLM在作者画像任务上表现出非平凡性能，但显示出系统性文化对齐：大多数模型默认偏向北美种族，而DeepSeek-1.5B更偏向亚洲种族。Ministral-8B在评估模型中表现出最强的种族偏见，而Gemma-12B显示出最平衡的行为。

Conclusion: LLM在零样本作者画像任务中表现出显著的文化偏见，需要开发公平性评估指标和缓解策略。研究强调了在部署LLM时考虑文化代表性的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in applications with societal impact, raising concerns about the cultural biases they encode. We probe these representations by evaluating whether LLMs can perform author profiling from song lyrics in a zero-shot setting, inferring singers' gender and ethnicity without task-specific fine-tuning. Across several open-source models evaluated on more than 10,000 lyrics, we find that LLMs achieve non-trivial profiling performance but demonstrate systematic cultural alignment: most models default toward North American ethnicity, while DeepSeek-1.5B aligns more strongly with Asian ethnicity. This finding emerges from both the models' prediction distributions and an analysis of their generated rationales. To quantify these disparities, we introduce two fairness metrics, Modality Accuracy Divergence (MAD) and Recall Divergence (RD), and show that Ministral-8B displays the strongest ethnicity bias among the evaluated models, whereas Gemma-12B shows the most balanced behavior. Our code is available on GitHub (https://github.com/ValentinLafargue/CulturalProbingLLM).

</details>


### [53] [TurnWise: The Gap between Single- and Multi-turn Language Model Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16759)
*Victoria Graf,Valentina Pyatkin,Nouha Dziri,Nathan Lambert,Hannaneh Hajishirzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TurnWiseEval基准和TurnWiseData管道填补了多轮对话训练与评估的空白，实验表明少量多轮数据即可显著提升模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开放训练和评估数据主要关注单轮设置，无法捕捉多轮对话的额外维度，存在多轮/单轮能力差距

Method: 1) 引入TurnWiseEval基准，通过成对比较等效的单轮设置来隔离多轮特定对话能力；2) 开发TurnWiseData合成多轮数据管道，可扩展生成多轮训练数据

Result: 使用Olmo 3的实验表明：多轮数据训练对实现强大的多轮聊天性能至关重要；在后续训练中仅包含1万次多轮对话即可在TurnWiseEval上带来12%的性能提升

Conclusion: 多轮对话能力需要专门的多轮数据训练，TurnWiseEval和TurnWiseData为解决多轮对话评估和训练数据稀缺问题提供了有效方案

Abstract: Multi-turn conversations are a common and critical mode of language model interaction. However, current open training and evaluation data focus on single-turn settings, failing to capture the additional dimension of these longer interactions. To understand this multi-/single-turn gap, we first introduce a new benchmark, TurnWiseEval, for multi-turn capabilities that is directly comparable to single-turn chat evaluation. Our evaluation isolates multi-turn specific conversational ability through pairwise comparison to equivalent single-turn settings. We additionally introduce our synthetic multi-turn data pipeline TurnWiseData which allows the scalable generation of multi-turn training data. Our experiments with Olmo 3 show that training with multi-turn data is vital to achieving strong multi-turn chat performance, and that including as little as 10k multi-turn conversations during post-training can lead to a 12% improvement on TurnWiseEval.

</details>


### [54] [Mediocrity is the key for LLM as a Judge Anchor Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16848)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Asaf Yehudai,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了LLM评估中锚点选择对结果可靠性的影响，发现锚点选择至关重要，不当锚点会显著降低与人类排名的相关性，并提供了基准规模分析和锚点选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管"LLM-as-a-judge"范式已成为评估开放生成的标准方法，且为降低成对比较的二次方计算成本，流行基准如Arena-Hard和AlpacaEval使用单一锚点进行比较，但锚点选择对结果可靠性的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 在Arena-Hard-v2.0数据集上系统评估了22个不同锚点，分析了锚点选择对结果的影响，量化了锚点选择的效应大小，进行了功效分析以确定足够的基准规模，并提供了锚点选择指南。

Result: 发现锚点选择至关重要：不良锚点会显著降低与人类排名的相关性；常见锚点选择（最佳和最差性能模型）是较差的锚点，因为它们始终优于或劣于所有其他模型，很少能反映模型的相对排名；锚点选择的效应大小与评判模型选择相当；标准基准规模对于成对评估不足，无法可靠区分竞争模型。

Conclusion: 提供了可操作建议：通过功效分析计算了锚点评估所需的足够基准规模，发现标准基准规模不足；提供了选择信息性锚点的指南，以确保可靠和高效的评估实践。

Abstract: The ``LLM-as-a-judge'' paradigm has become a standard method for evaluating open-ended generation. To address the quadratic scalability costs of pairwise comparisons, popular benchmarks like Arena-Hard and AlpacaEval compare all models against a single anchor. However, despite its widespread use, the impact of anchor selection on the reliability of the results remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate the effect of anchor selection by evaluating 22 different anchors on the Arena-Hard-v2.0 dataset. We find that the choice of anchor is critical: a poor anchor can dramatically reduce correlation with human rankings. We identify that common anchor choices (best-performing and worst-performing models) make poor anchors. Because these extreme anchors are consistently better or worse than all other models, they are seldom indicative of the relative ranking of the models. We further quantify the effect size of anchor selection, showing it is comparable to the selection of a judge model. We conclude with actionable recommendations. First, we conduct a power analysis, and compute sufficient benchmark sizes for anchor-based evaluation, finding that standard benchmark sizes are insufficient for pairwise evaluation and fail to distinguish between competitive models reliably. Second, we provide guidelines for selecting informative anchors to ensure reliable and efficient evaluation practices.

</details>


### [55] [Online Experiential Learning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16856)
*Tianzhu Ye,Li Dong,Qingxiu Dong,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出在线体验学习(OEL)框架，使大语言模型能够从实际部署经验中持续改进，通过提取可迁移的体验知识并进行策略上下文蒸馏，形成在线学习循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前改进大语言模型主要依赖离线训练和人工标注或模拟环境，忽略了实际部署中积累的丰富经验。这些部署经验是宝贵的改进资源，但现有方法未能有效利用。

Method: OEL框架包含两个阶段：1)从用户端收集的交互轨迹中提取和积累可迁移的体验知识；2)通过策略上下文蒸馏将这些知识整合到模型参数中，无需访问用户端环境。这两个阶段迭代形成在线学习循环。

Result: 在基于文本的游戏环境中，OEL在不同模型规模和思考/非思考变体上都实现了持续改进，提高了任务准确性和令牌效率，同时保持了分布外性能。分析表明提取的体验知识比原始轨迹更有效，策略一致性对有效学习至关重要。

Conclusion: OEL框架成功实现了语言模型从实际部署经验中的持续改进，证明了体验知识提取和策略上下文蒸馏的有效性，为语言模型的在线学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: The prevailing paradigm for improving large language models relies on offline training with human annotations or simulated environments, leaving the rich experience accumulated during real-world deployment entirely unexploited. We propose Online Experiential Learning (OEL), a framework that enables language models to continuously improve from their own deployment experience. OEL operates in two stages: first, transferable experiential knowledge is extracted and accumulated from interaction trajectories collected on the user side; second, this knowledge is consolidated into model parameters via on-policy context distillation, requiring no access to the user-side environment. The two stages are iterated to form an online learning loop, where the improved model collects higher-quality trajectories that yield richer experiential knowledge for subsequent rounds. We evaluate OEL on text-based game environments across multiple model scales and both thinking and non-thinking variants. OEL achieves consistent improvements over successive iterations, enhancing both task accuracy and token efficiency while preserving out-of-distribution performance. Our analysis further shows that extracted experiential knowledge is significantly more effective than raw trajectories, and that on-policy consistency between the knowledge source and the policy model is critical for effective learning.

</details>

### [56] [Global Stability and Step Size Robustness of RMSProp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15823)
*Naum Dimitrieski,Maria Christine Honecker,Carsten Scherer,Christian Ebenbauer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 为RMSProp优化算法引入输入到状态Lyapunov函数，证明其在恒定步长下的全局渐近稳定性，并建立对任意有界时变步长规则的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMSProp是深度学习中的关键优化算法，但缺乏严格的稳定性分析。需要建立理论框架来理解其收敛性和鲁棒性，特别是对步长变化的敏感性

Method: 引入输入到状态Lyapunov函数（ISS-Lyapunov函数）来分析RMSProp算法。该方法将步长变化视为系统输入，通过Lyapunov稳定性理论建立全局渐近稳定性和鲁棒性

Result: 证明了RMSProp在恒定步长下具有全局渐近稳定性，并建立了对任意有界时变步长规则的鲁棒性，为算法收敛提供了理论保证

Conclusion: 通过输入到状态Lyapunov函数框架，首次为RMSProp算法建立了严格的稳定性理论，为其在深度学习中的可靠应用提供了理论基础

Abstract: In this paper, an input-to-state Lyapunov function for the RMSProp optimization algorithm is introduced. Global asymptotic stability of the RMSProp algorithm for constant step sizes and robustness properties with respect to arbitrary bounded time-varying step size rules are established.

</details>


### [57] [Reachability Analysis for Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15865)
*Steven Nguyen,Jorge Cortés,Boris Kramer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出两种近似线性系统在有限时间内L∞范数控制下的可达集方法：第一种研究可达集边界，对单输入平面系统给出精确刻画；第二种利用Lp范数收敛到L∞范数，用Lp可达集近似L∞可达集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性系统在L∞范数有界控制下的可达集近似方法，为实际工程应用（如高机动飞机设计）提供理论基础，将可达性约束纳入设计过程以考虑物理限制

Method: 1. 研究可达集边界特性，对单输入平面系统（具有实不同特征值）给出精确刻画；2. 利用Lp范数收敛到L∞范数的性质，用Lp可达集近似L∞可达集；3. 通过最优控制结果简化Lp可达集计算

Result: 获得了单输入平面系统L∞可达集的精确刻画，提出了Lp可达集近似L∞可达集的有效方法，并将可达性分析成功应用于高机动飞机设计优化中

Conclusion: 提出的两种方法能有效近似L∞范数控制下的可达集，特别对单输入平面系统可获得精确结果，可达性约束可成功纳入工程设计过程以考虑物理限制

Abstract: We present an approach to approximate reachable sets for linear systems with bounded L-infinity controls in finite time. Our first approach investigates the boundaries of these sets and reveals an exact characterization for single-input, planar systems with real, distinct eigenvalues. The second approach leverages convergence of the Lp-norms to L-infinity and uses Lp-norm reachable sets as an approximation of the L-infinity-norm reachable sets. Our optimal control results yield insights that make computational approximations of the Lp-norm reachable sets more tractable, and yield exact characterizations for L-infinity with the previous assumptions on the system. As an example, we incorporate our reachability analysis into the design optimization of a highly-maneuverable aircraft. Introducing constraints based on reachability allow us to factor physical limitations to desired flight maneuvers into the design process.

</details>


### [58] [An Adaptive Method for Optimal Control Problems Constrained by Parabolic Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15895)
*Alexander M. Davies,Sara Pollock,Miriam E. Dennis,Anil V. Rao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应直接配点法求解抛物型偏微分方程约束的最优控制问题，通过变分重构、Kirchhoff积分变换线性化、hp翻转Legendre-Gauss-Radau配点法全离散，结合隐式残差估计进行误差评估和自适应网格细化，数值实验显示误差可降低五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决抛物型偏微分方程约束的最优控制问题时，传统方法在时空离散精度和计算效率方面存在局限，需要开发能够自适应调整网格、同时保证高精度和高效率的数值方法。

Method: 1. 变分重构：将PDE约束问题转化为变分形式，空间域采用hp-Galerkin有限元法离散；2. Kirchhoff积分变换：线性化变分形式中的非线性项；3. 时间离散：采用hp翻转Legendre-Gauss-Radau正交配点法；4. 误差估计：通过隐式残差估计程序评估局部误差；5. 自适应网格：基于Legendre多项式系数衰减率估计解的正则性，指导网格细化/粗化；6. 过配点区域采用网格缩减策略。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在空间和时间维度上都能将误差降低多达五个数量级，证明了自适应网格策略的有效性和方法的计算精度。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应直接配点法成功解决了抛物型PDE约束最优控制问题，通过结合变分重构、线性化变换、正交配点离散和自适应网格策略，实现了高精度和高效率的数值求解，为复杂最优控制问题提供了有效的数值框架。

Abstract: An adaptive direct collocation method is developed for solving optimal control problems constrained by parabolic partial differential equations. The partial differential equation is first reformulated in a variational setting, where the spatial domain is discretized using the hp-Galerkin finite element method. To address nonlinearities in the variational form, a Kirchhoff-like integral transformation is applied to linearize the dynamics. In the temporal dimension, an orthogonal collocation scheme, the hp-flipped Legendre-Gauss-Radau method, is employed to fully discretize the problem, yielding a large, sparse nonlinear programming problem. Upon solving the nonlinear programming problem, solution accuracy is assessed through an implicit residual estimation procedure. This approach evaluates the local error by solving auxiliary residual problems over selected subdomains, providing a novel means of error estimation within an orthogonal collocation framework for optimal control. Based on the computed error estimate, the mesh is adaptively refined or coarsened to meet a prescribed error tolerance. Mesh refinement is guided by the estimated regularity of the solution which is determined via the decay rate of the coefficients of a Legendre polynomial expansion. In overcollocated regions, a mesh reduction strategy is adapted from orthogonal collocation methods for application within the finite element framework. Numerical examples demonstrate that the proposed method can reduce the error by up to five orders of magnitude in both spatial and temporal dimensions.

</details>


### [59] [Parallel Newton methods for the continuous quadratic knapsack problem: A Jacobi and Gauss-Seidel tale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15910)
*Leonardo D. Secchin,Paulo J. S. Silva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了求解连续二次背包问题的半光滑牛顿法，通过整合Condat的高效初始乘子猜测和设计适应CPU/GPU计算模型的并行变体，显著提升了算法性能，并在开源Julia包中实现验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续二次背包问题在资源分配、多商品流、机器学习等领域有广泛应用，现有算法在效率和可扩展性方面仍有改进空间。本文旨在提升Cominetti等人提出的半光滑牛顿法的性能，使其能更好地适应现代计算架构。

Method: 重新设计半光滑牛顿法：1) 针对单纯形或ℓ₁球投影问题，整合Condat的高效初始乘子猜测策略；2) 构建灵活的算法框架，开发适应CPU和GPU计算模型的不同并行变体；3) 在开源Julia包NewtonCQK.jl中实现改进算法。

Result: 通过广泛的数值测试表明，改进后的算法在效率和可扩展性方面优于其他最先进的求解器。特别是在大规模问题上，新方法展现出显著的计算优势。

Conclusion: 改进的半光滑牛顿法为连续二次背包问题提供了高效且可扩展的求解方案，通过整合先进初始化和并行化策略，显著提升了算法性能，其开源实现为相关应用提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: The continuous quadratic knapsack (CQK) problem involves minimizing a diagonal convex quadratic function subject to box constraints and a single linear equality constraint. It has numerous applications in resource allocation, multicommodity flow, machine learning, and classical optimization tasks such as Lagrangian relaxation and quasi-Newton updates. In this work, we revisit the semismooth Newton method introduced by Cominetti, Mascarenhas, and Silva. We demonstrate that the method can be significantly improved in two directions. First, for projections onto the simplex or the $\ell_1$-ball, it can incorporate Condat's highly effective initial multiplier guess. Second, it can serve as a flexible foundation for CQK algorithms, allowing for different parallel variants tailored to exploit CPU and GPU computational models. These improvements are implemented in the open-source Julia package \texttt{NewtonCQK.jl}. We present extensive numerical tests comparing this implementation with other state-of-the-art solvers, demonstrating its superior efficiency and scalability.

</details>


### [60] [Fast Relax-and-Round Unit Commitment with Economic Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15934)
*Shaked Regev,Eve Tsybina,Slaven Peles*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于包含水电的长期机组组合问题的快速算法，可在商品硬件上1分钟内求解数千台机组的互联规模问题，显著降低运营成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进工具无法处理大规模互联系统的长期机组组合问题，需要开发能够快速求解包含水电的长期规划算法，以实现经济高效的运行决策

Method: 扩展了新颖的机组组合计算方法以包含长期规划，引入了针对水电机组组合的快速启发式算法，该算法可证明具有准确性，并利用现有连续优化求解器实现

Result: 算法可在商品硬件上约1分钟内求解数千台机组、5分钟市场间隔的互联规模问题，延长规划视野可带来显著的运营成本节约，这是现有工具无法实现的规模

Conclusion: 该方法使大型水电系统运营商能够做出考虑规划视野的经济决策，算法可适应不同应用场景，结合两种算法可实现大规模水电系统的经济优化运行

Abstract: We expand our novel computational method for unit commitment (UC) to include long-horizon planning. We introduce a fast novel algorithm to commit hydro-generators, provably accurately. We solve problems with thousands of generators at 5 minute market intervals. Abstract: Abel's classic transformation shows that any well-posed system with time-varying delay is equivalent to a parameter-varying system with fixed delay. The existence of such a parameter-varying constant delay representation then simplifies the problems of stability analysis and optimal control. Unfortunately, the method for construction of such transformations has been ad-hoc -- requiring an iterative time-stepping approach to constructing the transformation beginning with a seed function subject to boundary-value constraints. Moreover, a poor choice of seed function often results in a constant delay representation with large time-variations in system parameters -- obviating the benefits of such a representation. In this paper, we show how the set of all feasible seed functions can be parameterized using a basis for $L_2$. This parameterization is then used to search for seed functions for which the corresponding time-transformation results in smaller parameter variation. The parameterization of admissible seed functions is illustrated with numerical examples that contrast how well-chosen and poorly chosen seed functions affect the boundedness of a time transformation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入双核条件（DKC）正则条件来调控核函数的局部相对曲率。结合相对光滑性，DKC提供了梯度的对偶Lipschitz连续性：即使梯度映射在原始空间中不是Lipschitz连续的，在由镜像映射诱导的对偶空间中仍保持Lipschitz连续性。验证了DKC被多种流行核函数满足，且在仿射复合和锥组合下封闭。

Result: 建立了约束非凸相对光滑问题下随机重排镜像下降的首个复杂度界限和迭代收敛性。DKC条件被证明广泛适用于多种核函数，为分析动量方案、随机重排和方差缩减等高级优化技术提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: DKC条件填补了相对光滑性框架中的关键理论空白，为分析无Lipschitz光滑性假设下的高级优化算法提供了系统工具，扩展了相对光滑性框架的应用范围，使其能够支持更广泛的优化技术分析。

Abstract: The global Lipschitz smoothness condition underlies most convergence and complexity analyses via two key consequences: the descent lemma and the gradient Lipschitz continuity. How to study the performance of optimization algorithms in the absence of Lipschitz smoothness remains an active area. The relative smoothness framework from Bauschke-Bolte-Teboulle (2017) and Lu-Freund-Nesterov (2018) provides an extended descent lemma, ensuring convergence of Bregman-based proximal gradient methods and their vanilla stochastic counterparts. However, many widely used techniques (e.g., momentum schemes, random reshuffling, and variance reduction) additionally require the Lipschitz-type bound for gradient deviations, leaving their analysis under relative smoothness an open area. To resolve this issue, we introduce the dual kernel conditioning (DKC) regularity condition to regulate the local relative curvature of the kernel functions. Combined with the relative smoothness, DKC provides a dual Lipschitz continuity for gradients: even though the gradient mapping is not Lipschitz in the primal space, it preserves Lipschitz continuity in the dual space induced by a mirror map. We verify that DKC is widely satisfied by popular kernels and is closed under affine composition and conic combination. With these novel tools, we establish the first complexity bounds as well as the iterate convergence of random reshuffling mirror descent for constrained nonconvex relative smooth problems.

</details>

Method: 通过显式构造证明，对于具有阶段持续时间h的POMDP中的任何策略，都存在基础POMDP(h=1)中的一个策略能够保证相同的渐进收益。这种构造建立了两个模型之间的策略等价性。

Result: 主要定理表明：1) 任何在阶段持续时间h的POMDP中的策略都可以在基础POMDP中被模拟；2) 价值函数V(h)关于h是非递减的；3) 连续时间极限lim_{h→0} V(h)存在。

Conclusion: 本文建立了POMDPs中阶段持续时间参数h的全局行为理论框架，证明了策略的可模拟性和价值函数的单调性，为连续时间近似提供了坚实的理论基础，并确认了连续时间极限的存在性。

Abstract: Partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) with stage duration provide a framework for approximating continuous-time behavior by scaling transition probabilities with a stage duration parameter $h \in (0,1]$. While previous literature has primarily focused on the limit of the discounted value as the stage duration $h$ vanishes, this paper investigates the global behavior of the asymptotic value, $V(h)$, across varying stage durations. Our main result demonstrates that any strategy in a POMDP with stage duration $h$ can be mimicked in the base POMDP ($h=1$). Specifically, we provide an explicit construction showing that for any strategy in the POMDP with stage duration $h$, there exists a strategy in the base POMDP that secures the same asymptotic payoff. As a consequence of this theorem, we establish that the value function $V(h)$ is nondecreasing with respect to $h$, and that the continuous-time limit $\lim_{h \to 0} V(h)$ exists.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, scheduling problems of aircraft minimizing the total delays on a same runway and on dual runways are studied. Abstract: Coordinating large populations of autonomous agents, such as UAV swarms or satellite constellations, poses significant computational challenges for traditional multi-agent control methods. This paper introduces a new optimization framework for large-population control, termed occupation-measure mean-field control (OM-MFC). The framework models the evolution of agent populations directly in the space of occupation measures and casts large-population control as an infinite-dimensional optimization problem over measures, which becomes convex under a positive-semidefiniteness condition on the interaction kernel. A Frank--Wolfe (FW) algorithm and its fully-corrective variant (FCFW) are developed to solve the resulting problem efficiently, where each iteration reduces to a classical optimal control subproblem. Theoretical results establish convexity, existence of optimal solutions, and convergence guarantees of the proposed algorithms. Owing to its measure-based formulation, the framework naturally accommodates systems with very large numbers of agents. Numerical experiments on UAV swarm coordination and satellite constellation control demonstrate the scalability and effectiveness of the proposed approach in high-dimensional and constrained environments.

</details>

Abstract: We study minimax (generalized) solutions of a Cauchy problem for a (first-order) path-dependent Hamilton--Jacobi equation with co-invariant derivatives under a right-end boundary condition. Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized design approach of consensus protocols of multi-agent systems via a directed-spanning-tree(DST)-based linear transformation and the corresponding minimal communication links. First, the consensus problem of multi-agent systems is transformed into the decentralized output stabilization problem by constructing a linear transformation based on a DST of the communication topology, and thus a necessary and sufficient consensus criterion in terms of decentralized fixed mode is derived. Next, a new distributed protocol is designed by using only the neighbors information on the DST, which is a fully decentralized design approach. Finally, some numerical examples are given to verify the results attained.

Abstract: This paper investigates a decentralized design approach of leader-following consensus protocols for heterogeneous multiagent systems under a fixed communication topology with a directed spanning tree (DST) and asymmetric weight matrix. Abstract: Numerical optimal control is commonly divided between globally structured but dimensionally intractable Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) methods and scalable but local trajectory optimization. We introduce the Featurized Occupation Measure (FOM), a finite-dimensional primal-dual interface for the occupation-measure formulation that unifies trajectory search and global HJB-type certification. FOM is broad yet numerically tractable, covering both explicit weak-form schemes and implicit simulator- or rollout-based sampling methods. Within this framework, approximate HJB subsolutions serve as intrinsic numerical certificates to directly evaluate and guide the primal search. We prove asymptotic consistency with the exact infinite-dimensional occupation-measure problem, and show that for block-organized feasible certificates, finite-dimensional approximation preserves certified lower bounds with blockwise error and complexity control. We also establish persistence of these lower bounds under time shifts and bounded model perturbations. Consequently, these structural properties render global certificates into flexible, reusable computational objects, establishing a systematic basis for certificate-guided optimization in nonlinear control.

Abstract: We address optimal control of semilinear evolution equations on Banach spaces with finitely many control channels, a framework encompassing a broad class of infinite-dimensional dynamical systems, arising in many applications. For this setting, we derive an exact and global formula quantifying the increment of the cost functional with respect to an arbitrary reference control. This identity enables the design of monotone descent algorithms that require no linearization or step-size tuning. We further establish the existence of optimal controls and propose a practical sample-and-hold realization of the descent step suitable for numerical implementation. The effectiveness of the method is demonstrated on a controlled reaction-diffusion equation.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局抛物线松弛方法一次性计算所有抛物线，当容差较小或函数定义域较宽时，对应的混合整数线性规划规模过大而难以求解，无法与成熟的松弛技术进行有意义的比较

Method: 提出一种新颖的迭代方法来计算抛物线近似，该方法在所有容差-定义域组合上都能成功，而原始方法在这些情况下会失败。方法包括理论解释和分析，然后进行与分段线性松弛的计算比较

Result: 现代求解器SCIP在高容差时能更快求解分段线性松弛，但在更紧的容差下抛物线松弛表现更优。效果归因于问题规模差异：分段线性松弛需要引入二进制变量且数量随容差减小而增加，而抛物线松弛保持维度不变。对于包含(余)弦约束的问题，效果显著增强

Conclusion: 抛物线松弛在中等容差下优于SCIP独立求解，对于包含(余)弦约束的应用问题（如交流最优潮流），抛物线松弛是一种可行的松弛策略

Abstract: Solving mixed-integer nonlinear programs (MINLPs) typically relies on constructing relaxations that are easier to tackle than the original problem. Recently, global parabolic (PARA) relaxations were introduced, featuring separable quadratic functions -- paraboloids -- as global under- or overestimators of general nonlinear constraint functions. So far, the paraboloids are all computed at once by solving a mixed-integer linear program (MIP). For small tolerances or wide function domains, the corresponding MIP grows in size and is eventually intractable, reventing a meaningful comparison with established relaxation techniques.
  We therefore propose a novel iterative method to compute PARA approximations that succeeds on all tolerance-domain combinations where the original one has failed. The computational study is preceded by a thorough theoretical explanation and analysis. Finally, the improved method enables a computational comparison with piecewise linear (PWL) relaxations in terms of runtime on general MINLP instances.
  The results show that the modern solver SCIP can solve PWL relaxations faster when the tolerance is high, shifting strongly in favor of PARA for tighter tolerances. We attribute the effect to the difference in the corresponding problem size: PWL relaxations introduce binary variables to identify the active linear piece and their number grows with decreasing tolerance. PARA, on the other hand, does not require additional variables such that the dimension is maintained. For problems with at least one (co)sine constraint, the effect significantly amplifies. Thereby, for medium tolerances, PARA relaxations outperform SCIP stand-alone. Applied problems like alternating current optimal power flow (AC-OPF) feature such constraint types, leaving PARA a viable relaxation strategy.

</details>


### [72] [Liquidity Pools as Mean Field Games with Transaction Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16529)
*Agustín Muñoz González*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文将交易成本纳入流动性池的均值场博弈框架，建立了考虑交易费用影响的交易者库存动态模型，证明了均值场博弈解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前将流动性池建模为均值场博弈的基础上，本文旨在将交易成本（如池费用）纳入模型，更真实地反映交易者在恒定乘积做市协议中的决策行为。

Method: 扩展了先前均值场博弈框架，将交易成本纳入交易者库存动态，交易者基于经交易成本调整的中间价格操作，建立了新的DAI库存动态模型。

Result: 证明了考虑交易成本的新交易者博弈存在均值场博弈解，分析了交易者的优化策略和均衡存在条件，为未来研究提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 成功将交易成本整合到流动性池的均值场博弈模型中，建立了更完整的理论框架，为理解交易费用对去中心化金融中交易者行为的影响提供了分析工具。

Abstract: This paper extends the theoretical framework introduced in Liquidity Pools as Mean Field Games: A New Framework, where the interactions among traders in a constant product market-making protocol were modeled using mean field games (MFG). In this extension, transaction costs are incorporated into the traders' inventory dynamics, modeling the impact of pool fees on trading decisions. Traders operate at a mid-price adjusted for transaction costs, introducing a new dynamic for the DAI inventory. The existence of MFG solutions for this new trader game is established, taking these additional costs into account. The traders' optimization strategies and the conditions for equilibrium existence are discussed, providing a solid foundation for future research.

</details>


### [73] [An Extension of Major-Minor Mean Field Game Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16534)
*Agustín Muñoz González*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文将具有主导参与者的平均场博弈理论扩展到效用和成本函数不仅依赖于状态分布，还依赖于状态-控制联合分布的情况，并引入了主导参与者控制对系统动力学的直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具有主导参与者的平均场博弈理论仅考虑状态分布对效用函数的影响，但实际应用中效用和成本函数往往同时依赖于状态和控制策略的联合分布。此外，主导参与者的控制通常直接影响系统动力学和性能指标，现有框架未能充分捕捉这一特征。

Method: 1) 引入代表性智能体状态-控制对在共同噪声条件下的条件分布；2) 将主导参与者的控制u₀直接纳入系统动力学和泛函；3) 基于状态-控制对的条件分布重新表述优化问题；4) 通过随机最大值原理建立必要最优性条件；5) 推导耦合的SHJB-FP方程系统来综合均衡条件。

Result: 建立了包含状态-控制联合分布依赖性的扩展平均场博弈框架，获得了包含主导参与者控制直接影响的必要最优性条件，并推导出能够综合均衡条件的耦合SHJB-FP方程系统。

Conclusion: 该框架显著扩展了现有具有主导参与者的平均场博弈理论，能够处理更一般的效用函数依赖关系和主导参与者控制对系统的直接影响，为更复杂的多智能体系统建模提供了理论工具。

Abstract: This work extends the theory presented in Mean Field Games with a Dominating Player by Bensoussan, Chau and Yam on mean field games with a dominating player, to the case in which the utility and cost functions depend not only on the law of the states, but on the joint state--control law. We incorporate the conditional distribution of the state--control pair of the representative agent given the common noise of the dominating player. In addition, we generalize the role of the dominating player to include the direct impact of its controls $u_0$ on the dynamics and functionals of the system. The optimization problems are reformulated in terms of the conditional distribution of the state--control pair, the necessary optimality conditions are established via stochastic maximum principles, and a coupled SHJB--FP system of equations is obtained that synthesizes the equilibrium conditions. This framework provides a significant extension of the existing literature on MFG with a dominating player.

</details>


### [74] [Preconditioned Proximal Gradient Methods with Conjugate Momentum: A Subspace Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16573)
*Jian Chen,Xinmin Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种结合结构预条件子与低维子空间曲率信息的复合优化下降方法，通过二维子空间近似求解近端牛顿模型，实现高效收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决复合优化问题中线性算子结构预条件子与局部曲率不匹配导致的收敛缓慢问题，需要平衡结构利用与曲率信息

Method: 设计结构预条件子使近端子问题有闭式解，建立子空间近端牛顿框架，在二维子空间（预条件近端梯度方向+动量方向）最小化近端牛顿模型，通过Hessian度量正交化将二维非光滑子问题分解为两个一维优化

Result: 证明了方法的全局收敛性，在强凸条件下获得Q-线性收敛率，数值实验显示在高维病态问题上表现优异

Conclusion: 提出的子空间近端牛顿方法有效结合了结构预条件子与局部曲率信息，通过低维子空间近似实现高效计算，特别适用于高维病态复合优化问题

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a descent method for composite optimization problems with linear operators. Specifically, we first design a structure-exploiting preconditioner tailored to the linear operator so that the resulting preconditioned proximal subproblem admits a closed-form solution through its dual formulation. However, such a structure-driven preconditioner may be poorly aligned with the local curvature of the smooth component, which can lead to slow practical convergence. To address this issue, we develop a subspace proximal Newton framework that incorporates curvature information within a low-dimensional subspace. At each iteration, the search direction is obtained by minimizing a proximal Newton model restricted to a two-dimensional subspace spanned by the current preconditioned proximal gradient direction and a momentum direction derived from the previous iterate. By orthogonalizing the subspace basis with respect to the local Hessian-induced metric, the resulting two-dimensional nonsmooth subproblem can be efficiently approximated by solving two one-dimensional optimization problems. This orthogonalization plays a crucial role: it allows a single pass of alternating one-dimensional updates to provide a good approximation to the original coupled two-dimensional subproblem while keeping the per-iteration computational cost low. We establish global convergence of the proposed method and prove a $Q$-linear convergence rate under strong convexity. Comparative numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm, particularly on high-dimensional and ill-conditioned problems.

</details>


### [75] [Quantum-Enabled Probabilistic Optimal Power Flow with Built-in Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16780)
*Yuji Cao,Tongxin Li,Yue Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种量子比特高效框架，利用量子噪声提供差分隐私保证，解决概率最优潮流问题，仅需5个量子比特而非传统方法所需的600+个量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算在电力系统优化中面临量子比特有限和固有噪声的挑战，传统上被视为缺点的量子噪声实际上可以被利用来提供差分隐私保证。

Method: 首先将概率最优潮流线性化为多参数线性规划，将参数空间分解为具有预计算解映射的关键区域；然后使用变分量子电路根据每个不确定性实现分类关键区域并恢复最终解。

Result: 在69节点系统中仅需5个量子比特，相比直接量子最优潮流方法所需的600+个量子比特大幅减少；变分量子电路比其经典对应物提供2.1倍更小的隐私预算，在相同隐私水平下保持更低的不可行性和预测误差。

Conclusion: 量子噪声可以被积极利用来提供内置差分隐私保证，提出的量子比特高效框架能够有效解决电力系统优化问题，同时实现隐私保护与计算效率的平衡。

Abstract: Quantum computing has been regarded as a promising approach to accelerate power system optimization. However, challenges such as limited qubits and inherent noise hinder their widespread adoption in power systems. In this paper, we propose a qubit-efficient framework for solving a crucial power system optimization problem, the probabilistic optimal power flow (POPF). We demonstrate that quantum noise, traditionally viewed as a drawback, can in fact be leveraged to provide a built-in differential privacy (DP) guarantee. Specifically, we first linearize POPF into a multi-parametric linear program (MP-LP) with renewable uncertainties being the parameters. This decomposes the parameter space into critical regions with precomputed solution maps. Second, a variational quantum circuit (VQC) classifies the critical region based on each uncertainty realization and then recovers the final solution. In this way, the required qubits scale with the uncertain parameters instead of the network size, with only 5 qubits versus 600+ for direct quantum OPF in a 69-bus system. Moreover, we prove the depolarizing noise of VQC provides DP guarantees and characterize the privacy-cost tradeoff. Case studies validate the proposed VQC achieves 2.1$\times$ smaller privacy budgets compared to its classical counterpart. At matched privacy levels, the VQC also maintains lower infeasibility and prediction error.

</details>


### [76] [Exponential stability of data-driven nonlinear MPC based on input/output models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16808)
*Lea Bold,Irene Schimperna,Karl Worthmann,Johannes Köhler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 基于输入输出数据的非线性模型预测控制（MPC）方案，使用替代模型进行优化，证明了在足够长预测时域下的指数稳定性，并通过核插值验证了近似条件，用数值示例展示了实际应用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性MPC需要精确的系统模型，但在实际应用中往往只能获得输入输出数据。本文旨在开发仅基于输入输出数据的非线性MPC方案，使用数据驱动的替代模型来避免对精确系统模型的依赖。

Method: 提出基于输入输出数据的非线性MPC框架，使用数据驱动的替代模型进行优化。假设系统具有指数可稳定性和比例误差界，通过理论分析建立稳定性条件。采用核插值方法验证近似条件，并通过数值示例展示方法的实际应用。

Result: 证明了在足够长的预测时域下，基于数据驱动替代模型的非线性MPC方案具有指数稳定性。通过核插值验证了所要求的近似条件，数值示例展示了该方法对非线性系统的实际适用性。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了仅基于输入输出数据的非线性MPC稳定性理论框架，为数据驱动的控制方法提供了理论基础，并通过数值验证展示了其实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We consider nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) schemes using surrogate models in the optimization step based on input-output data only. We establish exponential stability for sufficiently long prediction horizons assuming exponential stabilizability and a proportional error bound. Moreover, we verify the imposed condition on the approximation using kernel interpolation and demonstrate the practical applicability to nonlinear systems with a numerical example.

</details>


### [77] [Prescribed-Time Distributed Generalized Nash Equilibrium Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16865)
*Liraz Mudrik,Isaac Kaminer,Sean Kragelund,Abram H. Clark*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出首个完全分布式算法，在用户指定的有限时间T内找到具有共享耦合约束和一般成本耦合的非合作博弈的广义纳什均衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏能在预定有限时间内收敛到广义纳什均衡的完全分布式算法，特别是在具有共享耦合约束和一般成本耦合的非合作博弈中

Method: 基于集中式梯度预定时间收敛结果，设计三层耦合动态：预定时间分布式状态观测器、基于梯度的优化律和双重共识机制，通过增益同步解决双向扰动，使用Fischer-Burmeister重构实现无投影算法

Result: 算法保证在预定时间T收敛到与集中式情况相同的变分广义纳什均衡解，数值仿真在纳什-古诺博弈和时间关键传感器覆盖问题上验证了方法有效性

Conclusion: 首次实现了预定时间完全分布式广义纳什均衡求解，通过三层耦合动态和增益同步技术解决了共识与优化之间的双向扰动问题，为时间关键应用提供了理论保证

Abstract: This paper proposes the first fully distributed algorithm for finding the Generalized Nash Equilibrium (GNE) of a non-cooperative game with shared coupling constraints and general cost coupling at a user-prescribed finite time T. As a foundation, a centralized gradient-based prescribed-time convergence result is established for the GNE problem, extending the optimization Lyapunov function framework to gradient dynamics, the only known realization among existing alternatives that naturally decomposes into per-agent computations. Building on this, a fully distributed architecture is designed in which each agent concurrently runs three coupled dynamics: a prescribed-time distributed state observer, a gradient-based optimization law, and a dual consensus mechanism that enforces the shared-multiplier requirement of the variational GNE, thus guaranteeing convergence to the same solution as the centralized case. The simultaneous operation of these layers creates bidirectional perturbations between consensus and optimization, which are resolved through gain synchronization that matches the temporal singularities of the optimization and consensus layers, ensuring all error components vanish exactly at T. The Fischer-Burmeister reformulation renders the algorithm projection-free and guarantees constraint satisfaction at the deadline. Numerical simulations on a Nash-Cournot game and a time-critical sensor coverage problem validate the approach.

</details>


### [78] [An Efficient Global Optimization Algorithm with Adaptive Estimates of the Local Lipschitz Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2211.04129)
*Danny D'Agostino*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: HALO是一种新的确定性分区全局优化算法，通过估计目标函数不同子区域的局部Lipschitz常数来计算下界并引导搜索，无需超参数调优，可识别重要变量，并能与局部优化算法耦合加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种无需手动调优超参数的全局优化算法，能够有效处理具有挑战性的现实世界黑盒优化问题，同时提供对优化问题的可解释性。

Method: HALO采用基于分区的确定性全局优化方法，通过自适应平衡全局和局部信息来估计局部Lipschitz常数（基于绝对斜率），计算下界并指导搜索。算法无需超参数，可识别重要变量，并能与梯度基和无导数局部优化算法耦合加速收敛。

Result: 在数百个测试函数上与流行全局优化算法比较，数值结果非常令人鼓舞，表明HALO能够扩展高效处理挑战性现实世界黑盒优化问题的工具库。

Conclusion: HALO是一种有前景的全局优化算法，具有无需超参数调优、可解释性强、能与局部算法耦合等优点，为处理复杂黑盒优化问题提供了新的有效工具。Python代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: In this work, we present a new deterministic partition-based global optimization algorithm, HALO (Hybrid Adaptive Lipschitzian Optimization), which uses estimates of the local Lipschitz constants associated with different sub-regions of the objective function's domain to compute lower bounds and guide the search toward global minimizers. These estimates are obtained by adaptively balancing the global and local information collected from the algorithm, based on absolute slopes. HALO is hyperparameter-free, eliminating the need for manual tuning, and it highlights the most important variables to help interpret the optimization problem. We also introduce a coupling strategy with local optimization algorithms, both gradient-based and derivative-free, to accelerate convergence. We compare HALO with popular global optimization algorithms on hundreds of test functions. The numerical results are very promising and demonstrate that HALO can expand our arsenal of efficient procedures of efficient procedures for challenging real-world black-box optimization problems. The Python code of HALO is publicly available on GitHub. https://github.com/dannyzx/HALO

</details>

### [79] [Neural-Symbolic Logic Query Answering in Non-Euclidean Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15633)
*Lihui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HYQNET是一个神经符号模型，利用双曲空间处理知识图谱上的复杂一阶逻辑查询，通过结合符号方法的可解释性和神经方法的泛化能力，更好地捕捉逻辑查询的层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱上的复杂一阶逻辑查询推理需要结合符号方法的可解释性和神经方法的泛化能力。现有神经符号模型未能充分捕捉逻辑查询的层次结构，限制了其有效性。

Method: HYQNET将一阶逻辑查询分解为关系投影和模糊集上的逻辑操作，使用双曲GNN在双曲空间中进行知识图谱补全，嵌入递归查询树并保持结构依赖关系。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，HYQNET实现了强大的性能，证明了在双曲空间中进行推理的优势。

Conclusion: HYQNET通过利用双曲空间表示，比基于欧几里得的方法更有效地捕捉逻辑投影推理的层次结构，为知识图谱上的复杂逻辑查询推理提供了有效的神经符号解决方案。

Abstract: Answering complex first-order logic (FOL) queries on knowledge graphs is essential for reasoning. Symbolic methods offer interpretability but struggle with incomplete graphs, while neural approaches generalize better but lack transparency. Neural-symbolic models aim to integrate both strengths but often fail to capture the hierarchical structure of logical queries, limiting their effectiveness. We propose HYQNET, a neural-symbolic model for logic query reasoning that fully leverages hyperbolic space. HYQNET decomposes FOL queries into relation projections and logical operations over fuzzy sets, enhancing interpretability. To address missing links, it employs a hyperbolic GNN-based approach for knowledge graph completion in hyperbolic space, effectively embedding the recursive query tree while preserving structural dependencies. By utilizing hyperbolic representations, HYQNET captures the hierarchical nature of logical projection reasoning more effectively than Euclidean-based approaches. Experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HYQNET achieves strong performance, highlighting the advantages of reasoning in hyperbolic space.

</details>


### [80] [NextMem: Towards Latent Factual Memory for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15634)
*Zeyu Zhang,Rui Li,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Wenjie Wang,Xu Chen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NextMem是一个基于自回归自编码器的潜在事实记忆框架，用于解决LLM智能体事实记忆构建中的上下文负担、灾难性遗忘和高成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实记忆构建方法存在局限性：文本方法带来沉重的上下文和索引负担，参数化方法面临灾难性遗忘和高成本问题，需要更高效的事实记忆框架。

Method: 采用自回归自编码器构建潜在记忆，确保准确重建；提出两阶段训练过程（自回归重建对齐和渐进潜在替换）；引入量化技术减少存储开销。

Result: 大量实验表明NextMem在检索、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面表现优异，实现了卓越的性能。

Conclusion: NextMem是一个高效的潜在事实记忆框架，解决了现有方法的局限性，为LLM智能体提供了更好的记忆能力。

Abstract: Memory is critical for LLM-based agents to preserve past observations for future decision-making, where factual memory serves as its foundational part. However, existing approaches to constructing factual memory face several limitations. Textual methods impose heavy context and indexing burdens, while parametric methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting and high costs. To address these challenges, we introduce NextMem, a latent factual memory framework that utilizes an autoregressive autoencoder to efficiently construct latent memory while ensuring accurate reconstruction. For better optimization, we propose a two-stage training process, including autoregressive reconstruction alignment and progressive latent substitution. We also incorporate quantization to reduce storage overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NextMem achieves superior performance, and excels in retrieval, robustness, and extensibility properties. We release our code and model checkpoints at https://github.com/nuster1128/NextMem.

</details>


### [81] [AIDABench: AI Data Analytics Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15636)
*Yibo Yang,Fei Lei,Yixuan Sun,Yantao Zeng,Chengguang Lv,Jiancao Hong,Jiaojiao Tian,Tianyu Qiu,Xin Wang,Yanbing Chen,Yanjie Li,Zheng Pan,Xiaochen Zhou,Guanzhou Chen,Haoran Lv,Yuning Xu,Yue Ou,Haodong Liu,Shiqi He,Anya Jia,Yulei Xin,Huan Wu,Liang Liu,Jiaye Ge,Jianxin Dong,Dahua Lin,Wenxiu Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AIDABench是一个用于评估AI系统在端到端复杂数据分析任务中性能的综合基准，包含600多个跨文档问答、数据可视化和文件生成的任务，基于真实场景数据，挑战性极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文档理解评估基准往往关注孤立能力或简化场景，无法捕捉实际应用所需的端到端任务有效性，需要更贴近真实复杂场景的评估标准。

Method: 构建包含600多个多样化文档分析任务的基准，涵盖三个核心能力维度：问答、数据可视化和文件生成。任务基于真实场景，涉及电子表格、数据库、财务报告和运营记录等异构数据类型，反映不同行业和岗位的分析需求。

Result: 评估了11个最先进模型（包括专有和开源模型），结果显示复杂真实世界数据分析任务对当前AI系统仍是重大挑战，最佳模型仅达到59.43%的pass-at-1准确率。提供了各能力维度失败模式的详细分析。

Conclusion: AIDABench为复杂文档分析任务提供了严格的评估标准，揭示了当前AI系统在真实世界数据分析中的局限性，为企业采购、工具选择和模型优化提供了原则性参考。

Abstract: As AI-driven document understanding and processing tools become increasingly prevalent in real-world applications, the need for rigorous evaluation standards has grown increasingly urgent. Existing benchmarks and evaluations often focus on isolated capabilities or simplified scenarios, failing to capture the end-to-end task effectiveness required in practical settings. To address this gap, we introduce AIDABench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating AI systems on complex data analytics tasks in an end-to-end manner. AIDABench encompasses 600+ diverse document analysis tasks across three core capability dimensions: question answering, data visualization, and file generation. These tasks are grounded in realistic scenarios involving heterogeneous data types, including spreadsheets, databases, financial reports, and operational records, and reflect analytical demands across diverse industries and job functions. Notably, the tasks in AIDABench are sufficiently challenging that even human experts require 1-2 hours per question when assisted by AI tools, underscoring the benchmark's difficulty and real-world complexity. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art models on AIDABench, spanning both proprietary (e.g., Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3 Pro Preview) and open-source (e.g., Qwen3-Max-2026-01-23-Thinking) families. Our results reveal that complex, real-world data analytics tasks remain a significant challenge for current AI systems, with the best-performing model achieving only 59.43% pass-at-1. We provide a detailed analysis of failure modes across each capability dimension and identify key challenges for future research. AIDABench offers a principled reference for enterprise procurement, tool selection, and model optimization, and is publicly available at https://github.com/MichaelYang-lyx/AIDABench.

</details>


### [82] [CraniMem: Cranial Inspired Gated and Bounded Memory for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15642)
*Pearl Mody,Mihir Panchal,Rishit Kar,Kiran Bhowmick,Ruhina Karani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CraniMem是一种受神经认知启发的多阶段记忆系统，通过目标条件门控和效用标记，结合有界情景缓冲区和结构化知识图谱，为LLM智能体提供稳定记忆管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统通常像外部数据库，具有临时读写规则，导致记忆不稳定、整合有限且易受干扰内容影响。需要一种更稳健的记忆架构来支持长期运行的工作流。

Method: CraniMem采用神经认知启发的门控有界多阶段记忆设计：1) 目标条件门控和效用标记；2) 用于近期连续性的有界情景缓冲区；3) 用于持久语义回忆的结构化长期知识图谱；4) 调度整合循环，重放高效用轨迹到图谱中并修剪低效用项目。

Result: 在长期基准测试中，CraniMem比Vanilla RAG和Mem0基线更稳健，在干净输入和注入噪声条件下表现更好，在干扰下性能下降更小。

Conclusion: CraniMem提供了一种有效的记忆架构，能够控制记忆增长、减少干扰，为LLM智能体在长期工作流中提供更稳定可靠的记忆管理。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in long running workflows, where they must preserve user and task state across many turns. Many existing agent memory systems behave like external databases with ad hoc read/write rules, which can yield unstable retention, limited consolidation, and vulnerability to distractor content. We present CraniMem, a neurocognitively motivated, gated and bounded multi-stage memory design for agentic systems. CraniMem couples goal conditioned gating and utility tagging with a bounded episodic buffer for near term continuity and a structured long-term knowledge graph for durable semantic recall. A scheduled consolidation loop replays high utility traces into the graph while pruning low utility items, keeping memory growth in check and reducing interference. On long horizon benchmarks evaluated under both clean inputs and injected noise, CraniMem is more robust than a Vanilla RAG and Mem0 baseline and exhibits smaller performance drops under distraction. Our code is available at https://github.com/PearlMody05/Cranimem and the accompanying PyPI package at https://pypi.org/project/cranimem.

</details>


### [83] [GSI Agent: Domain Knowledge Enhancement for Large Language Models in Green Stormwater Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15643)
*Shaohuang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GSI Agent框架，通过监督微调、检索增强生成和智能体推理管道三种策略增强LLM在绿色雨水基础设施领域的性能，显著提升领域特定任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绿色雨水基础设施系统需要持续检查和维护，但相关知识分散在市政手册、监管文件和检查表格中，非专家用户难以获取可靠指导。通用大语言模型缺乏领域专业知识，在工程场景中可能产生不准确或幻觉答案，限制了其在专业基础设施任务中的应用。

Method: 提出GSI Agent框架，采用三种互补策略：1) 在精心策划的GSI指令数据集上进行监督微调；2) 基于市政文档构建的内部GSI知识库进行检索增强生成；3) 协调检索、上下文集成和结构化响应生成的智能体推理管道。同时构建了与真实世界GSI检查和维护场景对齐的新数据集。

Result: 实验结果显示，该框架显著提升了领域特定性能，同时保持通用知识能力。在GSI数据集上，BLEU-4分数从0.090提升到0.307；在通用知识数据集上性能保持稳定（0.304 vs. 0.305）。

Conclusion: 系统性的领域知识增强能够有效将通用大语言模型适配到专业基础设施应用中，为解决绿色雨水基础设施领域知识分散和非专家用户获取可靠指导的问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Green Stormwater Infrastructure (GSI) systems, such as permeable pavement, rain gardens, and bioretention facilities, require continuous inspection and maintenance to ensure long-term performance. However, domain knowledge about GSI is often scattered across municipal manuals, regulatory documents, and inspection forms. As a result, non-expert users and maintenance staff may struggle to obtain reliable and actionable guidance from field observations. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong general reasoning and language generation capabilities, they often lack domain-specific knowledge and may produce inaccurate or hallucinated answers in engineering scenarios. This limitation restricts their direct application to professional infrastructure tasks. In this paper, we propose GSI Agent, a domain-enhanced LLM framework designed to improve performance in GSI-related tasks. Our approach integrates three complementary strategies: (1) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a curated GSI instruction dataset, (2) retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over an internal GSI knowledge base constructed from municipal documents, and (3) an agent-based reasoning pipeline that coordinates retrieval, context integration, and structured response generation. We also construct a new GSI Dataset aligned with real-world GSI inspection and maintenance scenarios. Experimental results show that our framework significantly improves domain-specific performance while maintaining general knowledge capability. On the GSI dataset, BLEU-4 improves from 0.090 to 0.307, while performance on the common knowledge dataset remains stable (0.304 vs. 0.305). These results demonstrate that systematic domain knowledge enhancement can effectively adapt general-purpose LLMs to professional infrastructure applications.

</details>


### [84] [QV May Be Enough: Toward the Essence of Attention in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15665)
*Zhang Edward*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从语言学和句法分析的第一性原理出发，推导出Transformer中QKV机制的本质，提出QV范式并验证其有效性，进而提出QV-Ka优化方案，为LLM架构演进提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer架构中的QKV机制缺乏从语言学角度的理论解释，且MQA、GQA、MLA等变体架构缺乏统一的理论框架来理解其有效性和权衡关系。

Method: 1. 基于词性标注和句法分析的第一性原理，从语言学角度推导QKV机制的本质；2. 建立统一的理论框架解释MQA、GQA、MLA等架构；3. 提出QV范式并通过实验验证；4. 基于QV范式提出QV-Ka优化方案并进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 成功从语言学角度解释了QKV机制的本质；2. 建立了能够统一解释MQA、GQA、MLA等架构的理论框架；3. 验证了QV范式的有效性；4. QV-Ka优化方案通过实验验证，显示出性能优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于语言学的QKV机制理论分析为Transformer架构提供了可解释的理论基础，QV范式和QV-Ka优化方案为大型语言模型架构的未来演进奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Starting from first principles and a linguistic perspective centered on part-of-speech (POS) and syntactic analysis, this paper explores and derives the underlying essence of the Query-Key-Value (QKV) mechanism within the Transformer architecture.
